1b1(rgyud): @#//spyan 'dren rgyud gsum pa/bla ma'i bla ma bla med dkon mchog gsum/che ba'i che 
1b2(rgyud): mchog dbang mchog dam pa la/gus pas phyag 'tshal dang bas skyabs su mchi//gnam sa'i mnga' 
1b3(rgyud): bdag skye dgu'i longs spyod gzhi//bstan pa dam pa chos kyi mgon mdzad pa//tshangs pa brgya byin rgyal chen 
1b4(rgyud): yul 'khor srung //'phags skyes mig mi bzang dang lus dan po//'jig rten skyong ba dbang po me lha dang //
1b5(rgyud): gshin rje bden bral chu lha rlung gi lha//rnam thos bu dang dbang bdag sa yi lha//nyi zla gza' skar phyogs 
1b6(rgyud): kyi glang po rnams//blon po sde dpon lha mo phyogs mtshams srung //btsun mo sras bcas so so'i 'khor 
1b7(rgyud): kun dang //lha yi sde dpon chen por mtshan grags pa//khyab 'jug tshogs bdag nan ti kar ti ka/nag po 
1b8(rgyud): chen po stobs chen stobs po che//gnod 'dzin pad ma pad ma chen po dang //dung dang dung chen gang po shin du gang //
2a1(rgyud): @#//mgrin bzang gdab bzang po nor bu bzang //bum rna dril rna rna gsum rna bo che//yang dag shes dang yang dag rgyal ba dang //rgyal ba'i khyu mchog 'brog gnas gangs la gnas//bde ri lngas rtsen lnga len tshigs lnga ser 
2a2(rgyud): mgrin bcu rnam 'jigs 'brug sgra nam mkha'i dbyings//stong gsum rgya mtsho dga' bo bsnyen dga' bo//ma dros gzi can nor rgyas gdon la 'jebs//drang srong rig 'dzin char sprin lo tog lha//lam lha nyin mtshan dus mtshams 
2a3(rgyud): tshe grangs yud//ha nu man ta la sogs mthu chen dang //tshig dbang lha mo sgra dbyangs dbyangs can dang //pe'u 'dra dpal gyi lha mo 'od zer can//dkar sham brjod skyob gang gA ya mu na//sras 'phan dung can ri mkhar nag mo dang //
2a4(rgyud): ma bdun srin mo bdun dang srid mo bzhi//nag mo chen mo ral pa gcig la sogs//lha mo chen mo gnod sbyin chen mo dang //srid mo chen mo sha za chen mo dang //gzhon nu'i sku 'tsho rdzu 'phrul dbang gis 'phags//slag chen brjid pa lha 
2a5(rgyud): dmag g.yul du bstod//rmang grags chen po'i dge bsnyen chen po dang //lha sras bzhon nu klu sras bzhon nu dang //rig 'dzin gzhon nu drang srong gzhon nu dang //bi k+Shus bsten pa'i gzhon nu lnga brgya spun//gnod sbyin gzhon nu tir 
2a6(rgyud): ka la sogs//gzi brjid chen po'i gzhon sde thams cad dang //skye dgu'i bdag po la sogs lha dang klu//gnod sbyin dri za lto 'phye lha ma yin//gser 'dab grul bum srin po mi'am ci//'byung po sha za yul bdag 'dre 
2a7(rgyud): la sogs//cang shes snying rje'i lam du zhugs dgu dang //rig pa'i lcags kyus zin pa kun gson cig/gang gi ming la'ang bkra shis dpal 'bar ba//yi ge gnyis 'brel dkon mchog gsum gyi mtshan//
2a8(rgyud): skyes bu gang zhig nyan tam 'don pa yang //gzhi'i gsang yun gyi chu srid 'thob 'gyur na//yon tan phun sum dpag yas dkon mchog la//bsnyen bkur mnyes byed bka' la gus pa dang //chos thos nyams su len pa ci 
2b1(rgyud): zhig smos//de bas gsung rab dpag bsam rgyas pa yi//lha yi longs spyod tshal du kun gshegs te//chos rje nyi ma thams cad mkhyen pa'i bka'//bdud sde lha min g.yul las rgyal byed pa//rin chen 'od mchog 
2b2(rgyud): lha dbang gtsug gi rgyan//bag yod dang ba'i spyi bos blang bar mdzod//kye rgyal ba'i sras khyod chur shog/bdag dang sems can kun kyang yongs su mya ngan las 'das par bgyis la/sems can ma rgal ba sam 'jigs par 
2b3(rgyud): bsod nams dang ye shes kyi tshogs bcal bas mi ngoms pa la sems zhog shig/mngon par 'du byed pa'i rang bzhin gyi mtshan nyid kyang ma gtang shig/sangs rgyas kyi gzugs kyi sku yang ma dor cig/'dod chags dang 
2b4(rgyud): bral ba'ang thob par gyis shig/chos thams cad la chags pa med pa'i thams cad mkhyen pa'i ye shes kyang btsal bar gyis shig/chos thams can la gzhan gyi dri nga 'jog pa med par sangs rgyas kyi zhing yang yongs 
2b5(rgyud): su spyad par gyis shig/zhing gi mtshan nyid nam mkha' lta bur yang shes par gyis shig/sems can yongs su smin par bya bas ma skyo shig/bdag med pa'i mtshan nyid kyang ma spong zhig/mngon par shes pa la 
2b6(rgyud): yang rnam par 'phrul par gyis shig/chos kyi dbyings las kyang ma g.yo shig/byang chub tu sems bskyed pa yang ma gtang shig/thams cad mkhan pa'i ye shes la yang 'byung bar gyis shig/chos kyi 'khor lo 
2b7(rgyud): bskor bas sems can thams cad tshim par gyis shig/chos nyid brjod du med pa las kyang 'da' bar ma byed cig/de bzhin gshegs pa'i rnam par 'phrul pa dang /byin gyi rlabs kyang yongs su ston cig/byang chub 
2b8(rgyud): sems dpa'i lus kyang mdor cig/skye ba'i skye mched thams cad du yang snang bar gyis shig/yongs su mya ngan las 'da' ba chen po yang yongs su ston cig ces/kye rgyal ba'i sras 'di dag ni lta ba'i chos zung 
3a1(rgyud): @#//
3b1(rgyud): byin rgyal chen lha dang klu//gnod sbyin dri za lto 'phyed lha ma yin//gser 'dab grul bum mi'am ci la sogs//sems can gang dag bde gshegs sras rnams dang //nor bdun phun sum dpal bzhed kun gson cig/sangs 
3b2(rgyud): rgyas bcom ldan yon tan bsam mi khyab//dam pa'i chos kyi yon tan bsam mi khyab/'phags pa'i dge 'dun yon tan bsam mi khyab//dkon mchog bsam mi khyab la mngon dad pa'i//rnam smin bla na med 
3b3(rgyud): pa'ang bsam mi khyab//bskal pa bye ba khrag khrig bgrang bar yang //dkon mchog mtshan thos tsam yang rnyed dka' bas//brgya lam lan cig khom ldan lus rnyed rnams//rdzu 'phrul dang ba'i shugs kyis 'dir gshegs te//
3b4(rgyud): rnam par grol ba'i rin chen sna tshogs gling //zab mo rgyal ba'i bka' la kun gson cig/bsngo ba gang dag dam chos lhur len 'dir 'dus pa//lha klu gnod sbyin la sogs mthu chen rnams//chos kyi bsod nams shes 
3b5(rgyud): kyang khyed bzhes te//gzi byin 'khor 'phel dgongs pa grub par bsngo//gsung rab bdud rtsi'i rjes su sgrubs mdzod la//gang la ci rigs so sor dpal bskyed cing //de bzhin gshegs pa'i bstan pa bsrung bar gsol//
3b6(rgyud): rgyud gsum pa rdzogs s+ho////rgya gar skad du/Ar+ya rang no la ka nA ma d+hA ra NI ma hA ya na sU tra/bod skad du/'phags pa dkon mchog ta la'i gzungs shes bya ba theg pa chen po'i mdo//sangs rgyas dang byang chub sems 
3b7(rgyud): dpa' thams cad la phyag 'tshal lo//'di skad bdag gis thos pa'i dus cig na/bcom ldan 'das rgyal po'i khab na/bya rgod kyi phung po'i ri la dge slong stong du tshang ba thams cad kyang dkar po'i chos yongs su rdzogs pa/seng 
3b8(rgyud): ge'i sgra chen po sgrog pa/ye shes kyi tshogs dpag tu med pa tshol ba la mkhas pa sha stag gi dge slong gi dge 'dun chen po dang thabs gcig tu bzhugs so//byang chub sems dpa'i dge 'dun chen po 'di lta ste/byang chub 
4a1(rgyud): @#//
4b1(rgyud): chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po blo gros rgya mtsho dang /byang chub sems dpa' sems dba' chen po 'byung dka' dang /byang chub sems dba' sems dpa' chen po dga' 'byung dang /byang chub sems dang sems dpa' chen 
4b2(rgyud): po spyod pa'i blo gros dang /byang chub sems dada' sems dpa' chen po spobs pa brtsegs dang /byang chub sems pa'i sems dpa' chen po mgyogs pa'i snying po dang /byang chub sems dga' sems dpa' chen po byams pa dang /
4b3(rgyud): de dag la sogs pa byang chub sems dpa' sems/dpa' chen po rnam par thar pa bsam gyis mi khyab pa la gnas pa/dpa' bar 'gro ba'i ting nge 'dzin gyis nges par 'byung ba/dbyangs sgra thogs pa med pa/sangs rgyas kyi zhing dpag 
4b4(rgyud): tu med pa zil gyis gnon pa la mkhas pa/mi 'jigs pa la gnas pa/rjes su chags pa dang khong khro ba dang bral ba/yid du 'ong ba'i dbyangs dang ldan pa/khams gsum la ma chags pa/g.yog 'khor mi phyed pa dang ldan pa/thams 
4b5(rgyud): cad mkhyen pa nyid las nges par skyes pa/ting nge 'dzin dang snyoms par 'jug pa tshad med pa dang ldan pa/re ba thams cad yongs su skong ba/shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa rtogs par khong du chud pa/lus dang ngag dang yid kyi las 
4b6(rgyud): kyi mtha' don yod pa la mos pa/thams cad mkhyen pa nyid la mngon par brtson pa/ting nge 'dzin dang spyod pa tshad med par byin gyis brlabs pa/mi 'jigs pa thob pa/stong pa nyid spyod yul ba/mtshan ma med pa la gnas pa 
4b7(rgyud): sha stag dang yang thabs gcig go/de nas byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po kun tu bzang po zhes bya ba 'khor de nyid du 'dus par gyur te/'dug pa nges bcom ldan 'das kyi zhabs la mgo bos phyag 'tshal nas 
4b8(rgyud): bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad ces gsol to//bcom ldan 'das chos kyi dbyings ji ltar rtogs par bgyi/bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/rigs kyi bu chos kyi dbyings ni dngos po med par rtogs par bya'o//rigs 
5a1(rgyud): @#//kyi bu chos kyi dbyings ni nam mkha' dang mtshungs pa dang /spros pa med pa dang ma spros pa dang bla na med pa dang dor ba med pa dang /dngos po med pa dang /gnas pa med pa ste de ltar rtogs par bya'o//byang chub sems dpa' 
5a2(rgyud): kun tu bzang pos gsol pa/bcom ldan 'das ci lags/chos kyi dbyings 'byung ba lags sam/bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/rigs kyi bu chos kyi dbyings ni 'byung ba med pa'o//rigs kyi bu chos kyi dbyings
5a3(rgyud): ni bsam gyis mi khyab pa'o//rigs kyi bu chos dbyings ni dngos po med pa rtogs pa ste/dngos po gang du yang brjod par bya ba ma yin zhing bstan par bya ba ma yin pa'o//byang chub sems dpas kun tu bzang pos gsol ba/
5a4(rgyud): bcom ldan 'das byang chub la rnam pa du mchis/bcom ldan 'das kyis bga' stsal pa/rigs kyi bu byang chub kyi rnam pa ni tshad med/de de ni tshad dang ldan par bstan par mi nus so//byang chub sems dpa' kun tu bzang pos 
5a5(rgyud): gsol ba/bcom ldan 'das ci la gas/chos kyi dbyings rnam par rtog par bgyid dam/bcom ldan 'das kyi bka' stsal pa/rigs kyis bu chos kyi dbyings ni rnam par mi rtog go/byang chub sems dpa' kan tu bzang pos 
5a6(rgyud): gsol pa/bcom ldan 'das gal te chos kyi dbyings rnam par mi rtog pa lags na/byis pa so so'i skye bo rnams ci'i slad du rtog par bgyid/bcom ldan 'das kyis 'ga' stsal pa/rigs kyi bur byis pa so so'i skye bo 
5a7(rgyud): thams cad ni ma rtog pa dang /rnam par rtog pa dang /yongs su rtog pa las byung ba'o//byang chub sems dpa'-i kun tu bzang pos gsol pa/bcom ldan 'dasde bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyi byang chub ni zab lags 
5a8(rgyud): so//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/rigs kyi bu de de bzhin te/ji skad smras pa de de bzhin no//rigs kyi bu gzhan yang chos thams cad spros pa med pa ni byang chub ces bya'o//de nas 'jam dpal gzhon 
5b1(rgyud): nur gyur pa 'khor de nyid du 'dus par gyur te 'dug pa des bcom ldan 'das kyi zhabs la mgo bos phyag 'tshal te/bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad ces gsol to//bcom ldan 'das kyis dkon mchog ta la la zhes bgyi 
5b2(rgyud): ba'i gzungs bshad du gsol//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/rigs kyi bu byang chub sems dpa' kun tu bzang po chos thams cad la thogs pa med pa'i spobs pa la gnas pa 'di la dris shig dang 'di 
5b3(rgyud): khyod la ston par 'gyur ro//de nas 'jam dpal gzhon nur gyur pas bcom ldan 'das la mngon du phyogs te 'dug nas sor mo bcu thal mo sbyar te bcom ldan 'das la 'di skang ces gsol to//thams cad mkhyen 
5b4(rgyud): cing thams cad gzigs pa bcom ldan 'das nyid kyis ci'i slad du mi bshad/bcom ldan 'das kyis bka'-i stsal pa/rigs kyi bu gang na byang chub sems dpa' yon tan 'di lta bu dang ldan pa dag 'khod pa de na 
5b5(rgyud): de bzhin gshegs pa rnams btang snyoms su bzhugs so//'jam dpal gyis gsol pa/bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa rnams ni 'dir btang snyoms su bzhugs pa ma lags so//bcom ldan 'das kyis bkA 
5b6(rgyud): stsal pa/rigs kyi bu nga ni sems can gyi khams yal bar 'dor ba ma yin te/rigs kyi bu 'on kyang byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi bstan pa ni bstan pa tshad med cing bstan pa bsam gyis mi khyab bo//de nas 'jam dpal 
5b7(rgyud): gzhon nur gyur pas yang bcom ldan 'das la 'di slad ces gsol to//bcom ldan 'das kyis dkon mchog ta la la zhes bgyi ba'i gzungs bshad du gsol//bcom ldan 'das kyis gaka' stsal pa/rigs kyi bu byang 
5b8(rgyud): chub sems dpa' kun tu bzang po 'di la dris shig dang /'di khyod la ston par 'gyur ro//de ci'i phyir zhe na/rigs kyi bu skyes bu dam pa 'di ni ye shes kyi tshogs la gnas pa'o//'jam dpal gyis gsol pa/gal te de bzhin 
6a1(rgyud): @#//gshegs pas bdag la dgongs na bdag gis skyes bu dam pa 'di la dri bar bgyi'o//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/'jam dpal khyod kyis ting nge 'dzin rdul phra rab kyi rdul snyed thob pa yin na de bzhin gshegs 
6a2(rgyud): pa la gsol pa gdab ci dgos/rigs kyi bu khyod ni sangs rgyas kyi chos thams cad la skal pa dang ldan pa yin no//'jam dpal gyis gsol pa/bcom ldan 'das bdag gis sangs rgyas kyi chos gang dag kun tu bsten pa dang bsgoms 
6a3(rgyud): pa dang mang du bgyis pa de thams cad ni de bzhin gshegs pa'i skyes bu'i mthur rigs par bgyi'o//bcom ldan 'da'as kyis bka' stsal pa/rigs kyi bu legs so legs so khyod kyis tshig 'di legs par smras te/'on kyang 'jam 
6a4(rgyud): dpal da khyed la gsol ba 'debs kyis khyod byang chub sems dpa' kun tu bzang po la dris shig/de nas bcom ldan 'das la 'jam dpal gzhon nur gyur pas 'di skad ces gsol to//bcom ldan 'das byang chub sems dpa' 
6a5(rgyud): kun tu bzang po 'di ni shin tu zab mo ston pa ste/theg pa chen po rtogs par khong du chud pa lags so//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/rig kyi bu khyod kyang chos thams cad kyi dbang phyug gi rgyal po yin gyis bdag la gsung 
6a6(rgyud): shig/yang rigs kyi bu khyod ni stong pa nyid la gnas pa'i ting nge 'dzin bsam gyis mi khyab pa dang ldan pa'o//de nas 'jam dpal gzhon nur 'gyur pas byang chub sems dpa' kunatu bzang po la sor mo bcu'i thal mo sbyar te phyag 'tshal 
6a7(rgyud): nas byang chub sems dpa' ku tu bzang po la 'di skad ces smras so//kye'i rgyal ba'i sras khyod kyis bdag la skabs phye na khyod la bdag yi ge 'bru gnyis shig 'dri'o//byang chub sems dpa' kun tu bzang pos smras pa/rigs kyi 
6a8(rgyud): bu de'i dus la dbab par shes na dris shig/de nas 'jam dpal gzhan nur gyur pas byang chub sems dba' sems dpa' chen po kun tu bzang po la 'di skad zhes smras so//kye rgyal ba'i sras sangs rgyas sangs rgyas shes bya ba'i gzhi'i 
6b1(rgyud): don gang yin//byang chub sems dpa' kun tu bzang pos smras pa/kye rgyal ba'i sras sangs rgyas kyi gzhi'i don ni gzhi med pa'i don to//sangs rgyas kyi gzhi'i don ni/dngos po med pa'i don to//sangs/rgyas kyi gzhi'i don ni bsam gyis 
6b2(rgyud): mi khyab pa'i don to//sangs rgyas/kyi gzhi'i don ni mi mnyam pa dang mnyam pa'i don to//sangs rgya kyi gzhi'i don ni spros pa med pa'i don to//sangs
6b3(rgyud): rgyas kyi gzhi'i don ni ma spros pa'i don to//sangs rgyas
6b4(rgyud): kyi gzhi'i don ni dor ba med pa'i don to//sang rgyas kyi gzhi'i don ni nam mkha' dang mtshungs pa'i don to//sangs kyi gzhi'i don ni bsnyad du med pa'i don to//rigs kyi bu sangs rgyas kyi chos rnams kyi rang bzhin
6b5(rgyud): ni de lta bu yin no//'jam dpal gyis smras pa/kye rgyal ba'isras gal te chos thams cad spros pa med pa yin na/'o na sangs rgyas kyi chos rnams/shes ji skad bstan/byang chub sems dpa' kun tu bzang pos smras pa/kye rgyal ba'i
6b6(rgyud): sras bstan pa 'di ni bstan du med pa'o//'jam dpal gyis smras ba/kye rgyal ba'i sras bstan du med na ci zhig bstan/byang chub sems pa'i kun tu bzang pos smras ba/'jam dpal bstan du med pa ni btags te bstan to//
6b7(rgyud): 'jam dpal gyis smras ba/kye rgyal ba'i sras btags pas ci zhig 'dogs par byed/byang chub sems dpa' kyi kun tu bzang pos smras la/'jam dpal btags pas ni dngos po yang mi 'dogs/dngos po med pa yang mi
6b8(rgyud): 'dogs so//'jam dpal gyis smrasapa/kye rgyal ba'i sras gal te btags pas dngos po yang mi 'dogs dngos po med pa yang mi 'dogs na/theg pa gsum po dag ji ltar bstan/byang chub sems de dpa' kun tu bzang bos
6b9(rgyud): smras pa/'jam dpal khyod chos kyi dbyings chags pa med pa la chags par ma byed/de ci'i phyir zhe na/'jam dpal de bzhin gshegs pas chos thams cad ni dngos po med pa'o zhes bstan pa'i phyir te/gang dngos po med
7a1(rgyud): pa'i phyir zhe na/phung po lnga dmigs pa med pa'i phyir ro//'jam dpal gyis smras pa/byang chub spros pa dang bcas pa yin nam/'o na te spros pa med pa yin/byang chub sems dpa' kun tu bzang pos smras pa/
7a2(rgyud): rigs kyi bu byang chub ni spros pa dang bcas pa yang ma yin/spros pa med pa yang ma yin te/'di ltar byang chub dang /spros pa dang bcas pa dang /spros pa med pa ni gnyis su med de gnyis su dbyer med do//de nas bcom ldan 
7a3(rgyud): 'das kyis byang chub sems dpa' chen po kun tu bzang po la rigs kyi bu bsam gyis mi khyab pa bstan pa 'di legs so legs so zhes bya ba byin no//gtam 'di brjod pas lha dang bcas pa'i 'jig rten 'di 
7a4(rgyud): la rmongs par 'gyur ro//'jam dpal gyis gsol pa/bcom ldan 'das chos bstan pa 'di lta bu ni rnam par 'tshal bar mi rung ngo //bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/rigs kyi bu de de bzhin te/ji skad smras pa 
7a5(rgyud): bzhin no//de nas bcom ldan 'das la 'jam dpal gzhon nur gyur pas 'di skad ces gsol to//bcom ldan 'das chos thams cad btags pa lags sam/bcom ldan 'das kyis bka'-i stsal pa/rigs kyi bu de 
7a6(rgyud): bzhin te/ji skad smras pa/bzhin no//de nas bcom lnga na 'das la byang chub sems dpa' kun tu bzang pos 'di skad ces gsol to////bcom ldan 'das rnam par dag pa'i sgo 'di ni mos par bgyid dka' ba lags so//
7a7(rgyud): bcom ldan 'das kyis bka'aM stsal pa/rigs kyi bu de bzhin te/ji skad smrasapa bzhin no//de nas bcom dang ldan 'das la byang chub sems/dpa' blo gros rgya mtshos 'di skad ces gsol lo//bcom ldan 'das byang chub 
7a8(rgyud): sems dpa' kun tu bzang bos rnam par dag pa'i sgo bstan pa 'di legs par smras lags so//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka'i stsal pa/rigs kyi bu de de bzhin te/ji skad smras pa bzhin no//rigs kyi bu gzhan yang 'di ni 
7b1(rgyud): chos thams cad kyi rgyu 'thun pa yin no//'di ni chos kyi char chen po yin no//de nas shing sa la chen po lta bu'i bram ze mnyam pa nyid du gnas pas bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad ces gsol to//bcom ldan 'das byang chub 
7b2(rgyud): ni na nyam pa nyid bsam gyis mi khyab pa lags te/de la yi ge mi dmigs/tshig 'bru mi dmigs so//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/rigs kyi bu de de bzhin te/chos kyi dbyings la tshig 'bru phyi snang /don kyang mi snang 
7b3(rgyud): ngo //de nas bcom ldan 'das la 'jam dpal gzhon nur gyur pas 'di skad ces gsol to/bcom ldan 'das stong pa nyid kyi rgyu 'thun pa gang lags/rnam pa gang lags/rtags gang lags mtshan ma gang lags/bcom 
7b4(rgyud): ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal ba/'jam dpal stong pa nyid ni brjod du med pa/bsnyad du med pa ste/brjod du med pa'i chos nyid 'di la mos pa gang yin pa de stong pa nyid ces bya'o//stong pa nyid de ni yi ge med pa ste/de'i phyir 
7b5(rgyud): stong pa nyid ces bya'o//stob pa nyid de ni bsnyad du med pa ste/de'i phyir stong pa nyid ces bya'o/rigs kyi bu chos thams cad ni ngo bo nyid kyis stong ngo //de nas bcom ldan 
7b6(rgyud): 'das la tshe dang ldan pa shA ra dwa ti'i bus 'di skad ces gsol 
7b7(rgyud): to//bcom ldan 'das byang chub sems dpa' bsam gyis mi khyab pa'i chos la gnas pa rnams kyis rnam par 'phrul ba la gzigs/bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/tshe dang ldan pa shA ra dA ti'i bu sems dang po bskyed pa'i 
7b8(rgyud): byang chub sems dpa'i shes pa ni mchog yin gyi dgra bcom pa'i shes pa ni de ltar ma yin no//de ci'i phyir zhe na/dgra bcom pa ni sangs rgyas kyi chos rnams las ring du gnas la/byang chub sems dpa' de ni sangs rgyas bcom 
7b9(rgyud): ldan 'das su 'gyur ba'i phyir ro//de nas bcom ldan 'das la byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po chos thams cad kyi dbang phyug gi rgyal pos 'di skad ces gsol to//bcom ldan 'das ji ltar bcom ldan 'das 
8a1(rgyud): @#//kyis gsungs pa'i don bdag gis 'tshal ba ltar na nyan thos kyis ni nyan thos kyi chos rnams ma thob pa nyid lags so//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/rigs kyi bu nyan thos kyis nyan thos kyi chos rnams 
8a2(rgyud): ma thob pa ni ma yin te/'on kyang nyan thos la byang chub sems dpa' dang lhan cig tu dris pa la lan gdab pa'am/spyod pa byin gyis brlabs par bya ba'i nus pa dang mthu gal yod/de nas bcom ldan 'das la 'jam dpal gzhon 
8a3(rgyud): nur gyur pas 'di skad ces gsol to//bcom ldan 'das shA ra dwa ti'i bu 'di ni de bzhin gshegs pas shes rab dang ldan pa rnams kyi mchog tu bstan pa lags na bstan pa 'di gang la bstan pa lags/bcom ldan 'das kyis 
8a4(rgyud): bka' stsal pa/'jam dpal ngas gang bstan pa 'di ni bstan pa med pa'o//de nas 'jam dpal gzhon nur gyur pas gnas brtan shA ra dwa ti'i bu la 'di skad ces smras so//gnas brtan gyis nyan thos kyi chos rnams thob bam/
8a5(rgyud): smras pa/de ni ma yin no//'jam dpal gyis smras pa/'o na ci khyod so so'i skye bo'am/smras pa/de ni ma yin no//'jam dpal gyis smras pa//'o na ci khyod slob pa'am/smras pa de ni ma yin no//'jam pa dpal gyis smras pa/
8a6(rgyud): 'o na khyod ci shes rab dang ldan pa'i mchog yin nam/smras pa/de ni ma yi na no//'jam dpal gyis smas pa/gal te btsun pa shA ra dwa ti'i bu so so'i skye bo yang ma yin/slob pa yang ma yin/shes rab dang ldan pa'i mchod kyang 
8a7(rgyud): ma yin na de nyid khyod dmigs pa can du 'gyur ro//shA ra dwa ti'i bus smras pa/rigs kyi bu kho bo ni skyes bu dam pa rgya mtsho ltar gting dpag dka' ba khyod dang 'gyed par mi byed do//'jam dpal gyis smras ba/btsun pa shA ra dwa ti'i 
8a8(rgyud): bu khyod na rgan rabs thu bo mchog yin gyis btsun pa shA ra dwa ti 'i bu de skad ma zer cig/gnas brtan gyis smras pa/rigs kyi bu rgas pa'i dngos pos ni ci yang 'thob par mi 'gyur/mngon par rtogs par mi 'gyur ro//rigs kyi 
8b1(rgyud): bu don de nyid shes par bya ba'i phyir khyod la dpe bya'o//rigs kyi bu 'di lta ste dper na rdo rje ngan don gyis kyang ri brag chen po rnam par 'jig go/rigs kyi bu de bzhin du khyod kyi spu'i khung bu gcig na shes rab 'dug pa gang yin 
8b2(rgyud): pa de ni kho bo lta bu'i sems can rdul phra rab kyi rdul snyed rnams la med do//rigs kyi bu don de nyid shes par bya'o//la dpe bya'o//rigs kyi bu' 'di lta ste dper na ba lang glang po che shin du mi bzad pa chen po 
8b3(rgyud): de yang mi stobs dang ldan pas lcags kyu chung dus dul bar byed do//rigs kyi bu de bzhin du/khyod ni stobs dang ldan pa/kho bo ni nyam chung ba ste/glang po che chen po 'di lta bu dang kho bo 'gyed ga la nus/de nas bcom ldan 'das 
8b4(rgyud): la gnas brtan shA ra dwa ti'i bus 'di skad ces gsol to//bcom ldan 'das 'di lta ste/dper na mi dmus long gis shul du mchi ba 'am/grong khyer mthong bar yang rdo mi thogs na khyim nas khyim du mchi ba'i rdo ltag la thog 
8b5(rgyud): ste/de ni gnas ma mchis so//de bzhin du glang po che chen po lta bu 'di dag gi 'dun na bdag ni dmus long dang mtshungs par mthong ste/sangs rgyas kyi chos 'di dag ni de tsam du rgya che ba lags so//bdag ni sangs rgyas kyi chos 
8b6(rgyud): rnams kyi snod du ma gyur te/da ci bgyir yang ma mchis so//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/shA ra dwa ti'i bu de skad ma zer cig/shA ra dwa ti'i bu 'di lta ste dper na de bzhin gshegs pa'i 'od kyis sems can ji 
8b7(rgyud): snyed cig la reg pa de dag thams cad sangs rgyas kyi chos rnams thob pa yin no//shA ra dwa ti'i bu de bzhin du khyod ni ting nge 'dzin mtha' yas shing bsam gyis mi khyab pa thob pa yin no//bcom ldan 'das kyis chos kyi rnam 
8b8(rgyud): grangs 'di bshad pa na/lha dang mi'i skye dgu las sems can dgu khri nyis stong gis chos mngon par rtogs par gyur to//de nas byang chub sems dpa' chos kyi blo gros byang chub sems dpa'i ting nge 'dzin dkon mchog ta 
9a1(rgyud): @#//la la sbyor ba mtha' yas pa zhes bya ba de la snyoms par zhugs so//byang chub sems dpa' chos kyi blo gros snyoms par zhugs ma thag tu phyogs bcu'i phyogs re re'i 'jig rten gyi khams sangs rgyas kyi zhing stong 
9a2(rgyud): phrag bcu'i rdul phra rab kyi rdul snyed kyi pha rol nas sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das sangs rgyas kyi zhing stong phrag bcu'i rdul phra rab gyi rdul snyed kyis/zhal mngon par bstan te/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das/de dag thams cad kyang 
9a3(rgyud): mtshan rdo rje'i snying po zhes bya bar 'thun pa sha stag go/phyogs gcig ji lta bar phyogs bcu rnams nas kyang de bzhin te/sangs rgyas bcom dang ldan 'das de dag gis de la 'di skad ces bka' stsal to//chos kyi blo gros khyod de 
9a4(rgyud): ltar byang chub sems dpa'i ting nge 'dzin sbyor ba mtha' yas pa la snyoms par zhugs pa legs so legs so//rigs kyi bu 'di lta ste/bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa rnam par snang mdzad 'di nyid kyi sngon gyi smon lam dang /
9a5(rgyud): sngon gyi byin gyi rlabs dang /khyod nyid kyi dge ba'i rtsa ba nye bar bstan pas 'di lta ste chos thams cad bstan par bya ba dang /sangs rgyas kyi ye shes rnam par sbyang ba dang /sangs rgyas kyi yi shes rnam par 'phel bar bya ba dang /chos kyi 
9a6(rgyud): dbyings su 'jug par bya ba dang /sems can gyi khams rnam par dgrol ba dang /chags pa med pa'i ye shes la 'jug cing chud par bya ba dang /kun du sbyor bar bya ba dang /skad thams cad brjod par bya ba dang /thams cad mkhyen 
9a7(rgyud): pa'i ye shes la 'jug par bya ba dang /chos thams cad la sgrib pa med par bya ba dang /dus gsum thams cad yongs su shes pa'i chos bstan pa la 'jug par bya ba'i phyir phyogs bcu nas de bzhin gshegs pa sangs rgyas 
9a8(rgyud): kyi zhing stong phrag bcu'i rdul phra rab kyi rdul snyed thams cad kyang mtshan mthun pa 'di dag thams cad khyod la byin gyis rlob kyis rigs kyi bu 'di lta ste/byang chub sems dpa' rnam par gzhag pa bcu las brtsams te/sangs rgyas 
9b1(rgyud): kyi mthu dang sangs rgyas kyi byin gyis rlabs kyis chos bstan pa 'di la spobs pa skyed cig/de nas sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das de dag gis byang chub sems dpa' chos kyi blo gros la thogs pa med pa'i ye shes kyi snang 
9b2(rgyud): ba dang /sgrib pa med pa dang /chod pa med pa dang /brjed pa med pa'i chos nyid dang /tha dad pa med pa'i ye shes dang /kha na mtho ba med pa dang /zil gyis mi non pa dang /zhum pa med pa dang /'phrogs pa med pa'i chos nyid 
9b3(rgyud): dang /brjod pa bzang po nye bar sgrub pa mdzad do//de ci'i phyir zhe na/'di ltar ting nge 'dzin 'di nyid kyi chos nyid thob pa'i phyir ro//de nas sangs rgyas bcom dang ldan 'das de dag gis phyag g.yas pa brkyang ste/byang chub 
9b4(rgyud): sems dpa' chos kyi blo gos kyi mgo la nyug pa mdzad de/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das/de dag gis byang chub sems/dpa' chos kyi blo gros la reg ma thag tu/de nas ting nge 'dzin de las langs nas byang chub sems 
9b5(rgyud): dpa' de dag la smras pa/kye rgyal ba'i sras dag byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi rigs 'di ni 'di lta ste/chos kyi dbyings kyis klas pa dang /nam mkha'i khams kyi mthas gtugs pa bas rgya che ba'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras 
9b6(rgyud): dag 'di ltar byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rnams ni 'das pa'i sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi rigs su skye ba yin/ma byon pa'i sangs rgyas bcom ldana'adas rnams kyi rigs su skya ba yin/da ltar 
9b7(rgyud): byung ba'i sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dis rnams kyi rigs su skye bayin no//de nas byang chub sems dpa' de dag gis byang chub sem dpa' chos kyi blo gros la 'di skad ces smras so//kye rgyal ba'i sras gang dag 'das pa dang ma 
9b8(rgyud): byon pa dang da ltar byung ba'i sangs rgyas bcom dang ldan 'das rnams kyi rig su skye ba'i byang chub sems dpa' sem dpa' chen po de dag ni gang yin/gang las bsams te de skad smra/kye rgyal ba'i sras dag byang chub sems 
10a1(rgyud): @#//dpa' rnams kyi byang chub sems dpa' rnam par gzhag pa bcu po de dag gang yin pa de shod cig/de skad ces smras pa dang /byang chub sems dpa' chos kyi blo gros kyis byang chub sems dpa' de dag la 'di skad ces smras so//
10a2(rgyud): kye rgyal ba'i sras dag byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi byang chub kyi sems dpa' rnam par bzhag pa ni bcu po 'di dag ste/de dag ni 'das pa dang ma byon pa dang da ltar byung ba'i sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyis gsungs so gsung 
10a3(rgyud): ngo gsung bar 'gyur ro//bcu gang zhe na/'di lta ste/sems dang po bskyed pa'i byang chub sems dpa' rnamapar bzhag pa dang /las dang po pa dang /rnal 'byor spyod pa dang /skye bar skyes pa dang /sbyor ba phun sum tshogs pa dang /bsam pa phun 
10a4(rgyud): sum tshogs pa dang /phyir mi ldog pa dang /gzhon nur gyur pa dang rgyal tshab dang /dbang bskur ba thob pa'i byang chub sems dpa'i rnam par gzhag pa'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras dag byangs chub sems dpa' rnams gyi byang chub sems dpa' rna ma 
10a5(rgyud): par gzhag pa ni bcu po de dag ste/de dag ni 'das/ma byon pa dang /da ltar byung ba'i sangs rgya bcom ldan 'das rnams gyis gsungs so//gsung ngo //gsung bar 'gyur ro//kye rgyal ba'i sras dang de la sems dang po bskyed pa'i bya chub 
10a6(rgyud): sems dpa' rnam par bzhag pa gang zhe na/kye rgyal ba'i sras dag 'di la sems dang po bskyed ba'i byang chub sems dpa' ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams mthong ma thag tu gzugs bzang ba'am/mdzes pa'am/kha dog bzang 
10a7(rgyud): po rgyas pa'am/rgya che ba'am/gzi brjid che ba'am/rdzu 'phrul gyi rnam par 'phrul ba'am/kun rdzob pa'i rnam par 'phrul ba'am/rjes su bstan pa'i rnam par 'phrul ba mthong ngam/sems can sdug bsngal bdag mthong ngam/de bzhin 
10a8(rgyud): gshegs pa'i bsngags pa rgya cher thos na 'di lta ste/rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa'i ye shes 'dod cing thog ma nyid du bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu sems bskyed do//sems bskyed ma thag pa de nyid kyis bsnyen par 
10b1(rgyud): dga' ba'i gnas bcu yongs su bzung bar 'gyur te/bcu gang zhe na/'di lta ste/gnas dang gnas ma yin pa shes pa dang 'das pa dang /ma 'ongs pa dang da ltar byung ba'i chos yang dag par blangs pa shes pa dang /thams cad du 'gro 
10b2(rgyud): ba'i lam shes pa dang /khams du ma sna tshogs shes pa dang /mos pa dang rnam par grol ba du ma shes pa dang /dbang po mchog dang mchog ma yin pa shes pa dang /bsam gtan dang /rnam par thar pa dang /ting nge 'dzin dang /snyoms par 
10b3(rgyud): 'jug pa'i kun nas nyon mongs pa dang /rnam par byang pa dang /ldang ba thams cad shes pa dang /sngon gyi gnas rjes su dran pa'i shes pa dang /lha'i mig gi shes pa dang /zag pa zad pa shes pa ste/bsnyen par dka' ba'i gnas bcu po de dag yongs 
10b4(rgyud): su bzung bar 'gyur ro//kye rgyal ba'i sras dag 'di ni sems dang po bskyed pa'i byang chub sems dpa' rnam par gzhag pa'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras dang /sems dang po bskyed pa'i byed pa'i byang chub sems dpa'i sems dpa' chen po de sangs rgyas 
10b5(rgyud): mchod pa dang /byang chub sems dpa' bde ba'i yo byad la sbyar bar bya'o//de la 'jig rten gyi bdag por 'gyur ba dang /mchog tu bya ba dang zil gyis mi gnon pa dang /sangs rgyas dpag tu med pa dang phreng ba dang /rab tu zhi ba'i ting nge 'dzin la rnal 'byor 
10b6(rgyud): du bya ba dang /'khor ba'i 'khor lo rnam par bzlog pa dang /dam pa'i chos kyi 'khor lo rab tu bskor ba dang /sems can sdug bsngal ba yongs su bskyab pa yang brjod par bya'o//de ci'i phyir zhe na/'di ltar sems skyes ma thag pa de nyid kyi shas cher sangs 
10b7(rgyud): rgyas kyi chos thams cad la sbyor zhing gong du gnas gang dag thos pa'i gnas de dag la yang bdag nyid sbyor gyi/gzhan gyi dring la mi 'jog pa thob pa'i phyir te/kye rgyal ba'i sras dag 'di ni sems dang po bskyed pa'i byang chub sems dpa' 
10b8(rgyud): rnam par gzhag pa'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras dag nga la las dang po 'di pa'i byang chub sems dpa'i rnam par gzhag pa gang zhe na/kye rgyal ba'i sras dga' 'di la las dang po pa'i byang chub sems dpas ni sems can rnams la thog ma 
11a1(rgyud): @#//nyid du sems rnam pa bcu bskyed de/rnam pa bcu gang zhe na/'di lta ste/phan par bya ba'i sems dang bde bar bya ba'i sems dang /snying brtse ba'i sems dang /mnyen pa'i sems dang /sdug pa'i sems dang /phan gdags 
11a2(rgyud): pa'i sems dang /kun du bsrung ba'i sems dang /snyoms pa'i sems dang /slob dpon gyi sems dang /ston pa'i sems te/sems rnam pa bcu po 'di dag skyed do//kye rgyal ba'i sras dag 'di ni las dang po pa'i byang chub sems dpa'o//
11a3(rgyud): kye rgyal ba'i sras dag las dang po pa'i byang chub/sems dpa' de lung mnod pa dang /kha ton la sbyar bar bya'o//mang du thos nas rab tu dben pa la sbyar bar bya'o//rab tu dben par gyur nas dge ba'i bshes gnyen la mngon par dga' ba la 
11a4(rgyud): sbyar bar bya'o//dge ba'i bshes gnyen la 'don par dga' nas dka' blo bde ba la sbyar bar bya'o//dka' blo bde bar gyur nas dus shes pa la sbyar bar bya'o//dus shes par gyur nas mi 'jigs pa la sbyar bar bya'o/mi 'jigs par gyur nas 
11a5(rgyud): don shes pa la sbyar bar bya'o//don shes par gyur nas chos kyi rjes su 'brang ba la sbyar bar bya'o//chos kyi rjes su 'brang bar gyur nas brjed pa med pa'i chos nyid la sbyar bar bya'o//rmongs pa med par gyur nas chos rnam par dgod 
11a6(rgyud): pa la sbyar bar bya'o//de ci'i phyir zhe na/'di ltar sems bskyed ma thag pa de nyid kyis shes cher sangs rgyas kyi chos thams cad la mngon par brtson par byed cing gong du gnas gang dag thob pa'i gnas de dag la yang bdag nyid sbyor gyi 
11a7(rgyud): gzhan gyi dring la mi 'jog pa thob pa'i phyir te/kye rgyal ba'i sras dag 'di ni las dang po pa'i byang chub sems dpa' rnam par gzhag pa'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras dag de la rnal 'byor spyod pa'i byang chub sems dpa'i rnam par gzhag pa 
11a8(rgyud): gang zhe na/kye rgyal ba'i sras dga' 'di la rnal 'byor spyod pa'i byed chub sems dpa' ni dmigs pa rnam pa bcus chos rnams rtogs par byed de/bcu gang zhe na/'di lta ste/chos thams cad la mi rtag pa nyid du rtogs par byed pa dang /
11b1(rgyud): chos thams cad la sdug bsngal ba nyid dang /bdag med pa nyid dang /stong pa nyid dang /g.yo ba med pa nyid dang /dbugs 'byin pa med pa nyid dang /gnas med pa nyid dang /rnam par rtog pa med pa nyid dang /rtsol ba med pa nyid dang /mngon 
11b2(rgyud): par 'du byed pa med pa nyid du rtogs par byed de/chos kyi rjes su 'brang bar gyur pa'i phyir sbyor bar mi byed mi sbyor bar yang mi byed de/kye rgyal ba'i sras dag 'di ni rnal 'byor spyod pa'i byang chub sems dpa'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras dag 
11b3(rgyud): rnal 'byor spyod pa'i byang chung sems dpa' de ni sems can gyi khams brtag pa la sbyar bar bya'o//chos kyi dbyings brtag pa la sbyar bar bya 'o//'jig rten gyi khams brtag pa la sbyor bar bya'o//sa'i khams brtag pa la sbyar par bya'o//
11b4(rgyud): chu'i khams dang me'i khams dang rlung gi khams dang nam mkha'i khams dang gzugs kyi khams dang gzugs med pa'i khams brtag pa la sbyar ba bya'o//de ci'i phyir zhe na/'di ltar 'di ni shas cher chos rnams la snang ba 'jug cing 
11b5(rgyud): gong du gnas gang dag thos pa'i gnas de dag la yang bdag nyid sbyor gyi gzhan gyi dri dang mi 'jog pa thob pa'i phyir te/kye rgyal ba'i sras dga' 'di ni rnal 'byor spyod pa byang chub sems dpa' rnam par bzhag pa'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras
11b6(rgyud): dag skye bar skyes pa'i byang chub sems dpa' rnams par gzhag pa gang zhe na/kye rgyal ba'i sras dag 'di la skye bar skyes pa'i byang chung sems dpa' ni yongs su sbyong bar byed pa bcus gsung rab 'phags par skyes pa yin te/bcu 
11b7(rgyud): gang zhe na/'di lta ste/chos nyid las phyir mi ldog pa dang /sangs rgyas la mi phyed pa'i dad pa mthar thug par 'gyur ba yongs su grub pa dang /chos nges par sems pa dang /sems can la rnam par dpyod pa dang /zhing la rnam 
11b8(rgyud): par spyod pa dang /'jig rten la rnam par dpyod pa/las la rnam par dpyod pa dag/rnam par smin pa la rnam par dpyod pa da /'khor ba la rnam par dpyod pa da/mya ngan las 'das pa la rnam par dpyod ste/yongs su spyod 
12a1(rgyud): @#//pa byed pa bcu po de dag gis gsung rab 'phags par skyes pa yin te/kye rgyal ba'i sras dag 'di ni skye bar skyes pa'i byang chub sems dpa'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras dag skye bar skyes pa'i byang chub
12a2(rgyud): dpa' de ni 'das pa'i sangs rgyas kyi chos mnyam pa nyid rnam par dpyad pa la sbyar bar bya'o//ma byon pa'i sangs rgyas kyi chos bamanyam pa nyid rnam par dbyad pa la sbyar bar bya'o//da ltar byung ba'i sangs rgyas kyi chos mnya
12a3(rgyud): ma byon pa'i sangs rgyas kyis tshes yang dag par 'grub pa rnam par dpyad pa la sbyor bar bya'o//da ltar byung ba'i sangs rgya sa kyi chos yang dag par 'grub par rnam par spyad pa la sbyar bar bya'o/
12a4(rgyud): chos yang dag par 'grub ba rnam par dpyad pa la sbyarabar bya'o//'das pa'i sangs rgyas kyis chos yongs su 'grub pa mnyam pa nyid rnam par pa la sbyar bar bya'o/ma byon pa'i sangs rgyas kyi
12a5(rgyud): 'grub pa mnyam pa nyid rna ma par dpyad pa la sbyar par bya'o//de ltar byung ba'i sangs rgyas
12a6(rgyud): 'grub pa mnyam pa nyid dam par dpyad pa la sbyar ra bar bya'o//de ci'i phyir zhe na/'di ltar de ni shas cher dus gsum mnyam pa nyid rtogs par byed cing /gong du gnas gang dag thos pa'i gnas de dag la yang bdag nyid
12a7(rgyud): sbyor gyi gzhan gyi dring la mi 'jog pa thob pa'i phyir te/kye rgyal ba'i sras dag 'di ni skye bar skyes pa'i byang chub sems dpa' rnam par gzhag pa'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras dag de la sbyor ba phun sum tshogs pa'i byang
12b1(rgyud): byang chub sems dpa' rnam par gzhag pa gang zhe na/kye rgyal ba'i sras dag 'di la sbyor ba phun sum tshogs pa'i byang chub sems dpa' ni dge ba'i las gang ci rtsom yang de dag thams cad sems can thams cad 
12b2(rgyud): yongs su bskyab pa dang /sems can thams cad la phan par 'dod par bya ba dang /sems can thams chad la bde bar sbyar ba dang /sems can tham cad la snying brtse bar bya ba dang /sems can/thams cad rab tu thar bar bya 
12b3(rgyud): ba dang /sems can thamasacad la gnod pa med par bya ba dang /semasacan thams cad drang bar bya ba dang /sems tsam thams cad dad par bya ba dang /sems/sems can thams cad gdul ba dang /sems can thamad cad yongs su mya 
12b4(rgyud): ngan las bzla ba'i phyir brtsom ste/kye rgyal ba'i sras dga' 'di ni sbyor ba phun sum tshogs pa byang chub sems dpa'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras dag sbyor ba phun sum tshogs ba'i byang chub sems dpa' de la gnas bcu yang 
12b5(rgyud): dag par brjod par bya ste/bcu gang zhe na/'di lta ste/sems can mtha' yas pa dang /sems can dpag tu med pa dang /sems can grangs med pa dang /sems cad bsam gyis mi khyab pa dang /sems can mtshungs pa med 
12b6(rgyud): pa dang /sems can gzhal du med pa dang /sems can/stong pa nyid dang /sems can g.yo ba med pa nyid dang /sems can/med pa nyid dang /sems/sems cad rang bzhin med pa nyid do//de ci'i phyir zhe na/'di ltar de shas cher mngon par
